```yaml
lines:
  - id: f2441.h_provider_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons or organizations who provided the care (entries on line 1)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dcprov, field: dcprov.one}
  - id: f2441.line1_box
    printed: "1 (box)"
    label: "If you have more than three care providers, see the instructions and check this box"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f2441.h_provider_count, {param: dc.providers_listed}]}
  - id: f2441.h_qp_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying persons"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [f2441.student_disabled]
    note: "The count is taken over the listed persons whose entry is a qualifying person (dependent.dc_qp). A spouse who was not able to care for themselves is also a qualifying person but is not an entry of that collection; that case falls under the screening question f2441.student_disabled, so it is refused here. The supplied text does not say whether a person described by f2441.nondependent is entered in the collection; this reading assumes every qualifying person other than a spouse is."
  - id: f2441.line2_box
    printed: "2 (box)"
    label: "Information about your qualifying person(s). If you have more than three qualifying persons, see the instructions and check this box"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f2441.h_qp_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
    refuses: [f2441.student_disabled]
  - id: f2441.h_limit_one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$3,000 (one qualifying person)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: dc.limit_one}
  - id: f2441.h_limit_two
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$6,000 (two or more qualifying persons)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: dc.limit_two}
  - id: f2441.h_dollar_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$3,000 if you had one qualifying person or $6,000 if you had two or more persons"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [f2441.h_qp_count, {param: dc.persons_two}]}
          then: f2441.h_limit_two
        - guard: {op: test, lt: [f2441.h_qp_count, {param: dc.persons_two}]}
          then: f2441.h_limit_one
    note: "The form gives $3,000 unless there are two or more qualifying persons; with no qualifying person this reading also gives $3,000 (the column (d) total is then zero)."
  - id: f2441.h_col_d_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add the amounts in column (d) of line 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.dc_line2d}
  - id: f2441.h_line3_part2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (d) total, not more than $3,000 if you had one qualifying person or $6,000 if you had two or more persons"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f2441.h_col_d_total, f2441.h_dollar_limit]}
  - id: f2441.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Add the amounts in column (d) of line 2. Don't enter more than $3,000 if you had one qualifying person or $6,000 if you had two or more persons. If you completed Part III, enter the amount from line 31"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f2441.benefits, {const: true}]}
          then: f2441.line31
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f2441.benefits, {const: false}]}
          then: f2441.h_line3_part2
    note: "Part III is treated as completed exactly when dependent care benefits were received (f2441.benefits). Line A (a married person filing separately may claim the credit only if treated as unmarried) has no input in the supplied interface, so that condition is not encoded here; the text and interface do not determine it."
  - id: f2441.h_line12_13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add the amounts on lines 12 and 13"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f2441.line12, f2441.line13]}
  - id: f2441.h_line14_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: line 14 (amounts included on line 12 that were forfeited or carried forward) is more than line 12"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f2441.line14, f2441.line12]}
    note: "The line 14 instructions say to enter the total of the listed amounts 'included on line 12', so line 14 cannot be more than line 12; inferred as a consistency check on the supplied figures."
  - id: f2441.line15
    printed: "15"
    label: "Combine lines 12 through 14. See instructions"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f2441.h_line12_13, take: f2441.line14}
    refuses:
      - {when: f2441.h_line14_over, when_known: true}
    shown_when: f2441.benefits
  - id: f2441.h_incurred_paid
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified expenses incurred and paid in 2025 for the care of the qualifying person(s)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.dc_paid
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
  - id: f2441.line16
    printed: "16"
    label: "Enter the total amount of qualified expenses incurred in 2025 for the care of the qualifying person(s)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f2441.h_incurred_paid, f2441.paid_2026]}
    refuses: [f2441.employer_care]
    shown_when: f2441.benefits
    note: "It doesn't matter when the expenses were paid: the total is the per-person care amounts for qualifying persons plus the 2025 expenses not already included in them (f2441.paid_2026). The value of care the employer furnished itself is not among the supplied amounts, so that case is refused."
  - id: f2441.h_smaller_20_21
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The smaller of line 20 or line 21"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f2441.line20, f2441.line21]}
  - id: f2441.line25
    printed: "25"
    label: "Excluded benefits. If you checked \"No\" on line 22, enter the smaller of line 20 or line 21. Otherwise, subtract line 24 from the smaller of line 20 or line 21. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f2441.h_smaller_20_21, take: f2441.line24}
    shown_when: f2441.benefits
    note: "No separate line is supplied for the line 22 Yes/No checkbox. With 'No', line 22 is -0- and so line 24 (the smallest of lines 20, 21 and 22) is -0-, and subtracting it gives the smaller of line 20 or line 21; one subtraction therefore covers both branches."
  - id: f2441.line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Enter $3,000 ($6,000 if two or more qualifying persons)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f2441.h_dollar_limit}
    refuses: [f2441.student_disabled]
    shown_when: f2441.benefits
    note: "Lines 27 through 31 are completed 'to claim the child and dependent care credit' by those who completed Part III; the interface has no separate line for choosing to claim the credit, so the line is shown whenever Part III is completed."
  - id: f2441.line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "Complete line 2 on page 1 of this form. Don't include in column (d) any benefits shown on line 28 above. Then, add the amounts in column (d) and enter the total here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f2441.h_col_d_total, take: f2441.line28}
    refuses: [f2441.employer_care]
    shown_when: f2441.benefits
    note: "The per-person column (d) amounts supplied include expenses paid with dependent care benefits, and a per-person entry cannot be reduced by the return-level line 28. 'Don't include in column (d) any benefits shown on line 28' is therefore applied to the total: the column (d) total less line 28, not below zero. How line 28 benefits would be allocated among persons is not stated in the text. Employer-furnished care, whose benefits are not in column (d), is refused."
```

Second transcription's note: The per-person column (d) amounts supplied include expenses paid with dependent care benefits, and a per-person entry cannot be reduced by the return-level line 28. 'Don't include in column (d) any benefits shown on line 28' is therefore applied to the total: the column (d) total less line 28, not below zero. How line 28 benefits would be allocated among persons is not stated in the text. Employer-furnished care, whose benefits are not in column (d), is refused.
